Title: Pia Desideria, oder hertzliches Verlangen nach gottgefälliger Besserung der wahren evangelischen Kirchen, sampt einigen dahin einfältig abzweckenden christlichen Vorschlägen; sampt angehengten zweyer christlichen Theologorum Bedencken
Creator: Philipp Jakob Spener (1635-1705)
Publisher: Frankfurt am Main: Johann David Zunner's heirs
Publication Date: 1706
Rights: Public Domain (published 1706, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: deu
Source: Internet Archive item 11303913bsb (https://archive.org/details/11303913bsb), the item's own 11303913bsb_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: not named in the item metadata; host metadata date: 1706. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-16. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: a00a63770084f3e91d85edaf5caf80a7d44d5b52149a0977c8484b4331211e6e.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Early-modern Fraktur OCR with the long s kept; letters drop in places. Second witness for exact wording beside the 1841 reprint.
Content note: A near-contemporary printing of Pia Desideria with the two theologians' appended opinions; dedicated to the pastors of the Protestant churches.

Spener, Philipp Jakob, 


Pia Desideria: Oder Hertzliches Verlangen, Nach Gotigefälliger Besserung 
der wahren Evangelischen Kirchen sampt einigen dahin eng 
abzweckenden Christlichen Vorschlägen, Philpp Jacob Speners, O. Predigers 
nd Senioris zu Franckfurt am Mayn; Sampt angehengten Zweyer Christlichen 
Theologorum darüber gestellten, und zu mehrer auferbauung höchstelenlichen. 


Franckfurt am Main 1706. 
Asc. 5567 y
